### [63] [Decentralized Task Scheduling in Distributed Systems: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24738)
*Daniel Benniah John*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于NumPy轻量级实现的去中心化多智能体深度强化学习（DRL-MADRL）任务调度框架，在异构分布式系统中实现了任务完成时间、能效和SLA满意度的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式系统中的高效任务调度面临动态工作负载、异构资源和竞争性服务质量需求的挑战。传统集中式方法存在可扩展性限制和单点故障问题，而经典启发式算法缺乏对变化环境的适应性。

Method: 将任务调度问题建模为去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDP），开发了仅使用NumPy实现的轻量级actor-critic架构，无需依赖重量级机器学习框架，可在资源受限的边缘设备上部署。

Result: 在100节点异构系统上处理每轮1000个任务的实验表明：平均任务完成时间缩短15.6%（30.8s vs 36.5s），能效提升15.2%（745.2 kWh vs 878.3 kWh），SLA满意度达82.3%相比基线75.5%显著提高，所有改进均具有统计显著性（p < 0.001）。

Conclusion: 轻量级去中心化多智能体深度强化学习框架能够有效解决异构分布式系统任务调度问题，在性能和资源约束之间取得良好平衡，为边缘设备部署提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient task scheduling in large-scale distributed systems presents significant challenges due to dynamic workloads, heterogeneous resources, and competing quality-of-service requirements. Traditional centralized approaches face scalability limitations and single points of failure, while classical heuristics lack adaptability to changing conditions. This paper proposes a decentralized multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (DRL-MADRL) framework for task scheduling in heterogeneous distributed systems. We formulate the problem as a Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (Dec-POMDP) and develop a lightweight actor-critic architecture implemented using only NumPy, enabling deployment on resource-constrained edge devices without heavyweight machine learning frameworks. Using workload characteristics derived from the publicly available Google Cluster Trace dataset, we evaluate our approach on a 100-node heterogeneous system processing 1,000 tasks per episode over 30 experimental runs. Experimental results demonstrate 15.6% improvement in average task completion time (30.8s vs 36.5s for random baseline), 15.2% energy efficiency gain (745.2 kWh vs 878.3 kWh), and 82.3% SLA satisfaction compared to 75.5% for baselines, with all improvements statistically significant (p < 0.001). The lightweight implementation requires only NumPy, Matplotlib, and SciPy. Complete source code and experimental data are provided for full reproducibility at https://github.com/danielbenniah/marl-distributed-scheduling.

</details>


### [64] [Rafture: Erasure-coded Raft with Post-Dissemination Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24761)
*Rithwik Kerur,Divyakant Agrawal,Michael K. Reiter,Dahlia Malkhi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Rafture，一种新型的信息分散算法，首次引入传播后剪枝机制，通过固定阈值纠删码和自主适应策略，在动态网络环境下有效降低存储开销并简化恢复过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠删码数据分发方案主要关注传播时的效率，存在恢复复杂、依赖集中式领导者决策、需要额外元数据等局限性，难以适应具有不可预测网络延迟的实际分布式系统环境。

Method: 提出Rafture算法，核心创新包括：1）引入传播后剪枝机制，允许系统在分发完成后调整存储成本；2）采用简单的固定阈值纠删码，沿第二维度变化片段分配；3）节点基于本地观察信息自主适应，无需全局协调。

Result: 在高度动态网络环境下的评估表明，Rafture在保证可恢复性（仅需F+1个片段即可重建，无需额外元数据）的同时，显著降低了长期存储开销，有效应对网络条件变化。

Conclusion: Rafture成功解决了现有信息分散方案的局限性，通过传播后剪枝和自主适应机制，在动态网络环境下实现了存储效率与恢复简单性的平衡，为共识协议中的信息分散提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Spreading and storing erasure-coded data in distributed systems effectively is challenging in real settings. Practical deployments must contend with unpredictable network latencies, particularly when information dispersal is integrated into consensus protocols, a prominent and latency-sensitive use case. Existing approaches address this challenge through timeout-based dissemination and adaptive communication or storage decisions driven by acknowledgments during dissemination. However, these designs focus almost exclusively on dissemination-time efficiency, complicate recovery with reconstruction procedures that require metadata that can differ per consensus value, and rely on a centralized leader to make storage decisions for all nodes.
  This paper introduces \textbf{Rafture}, a novel information dispersal algorithm, and its integration in a consensus protocol, that overcomes these limitations. Rafture is the first information dispersal solution to incorporate post-dissemination pruning, allowing systems to adapt storage costs after dissemination completes. It employs a simple, fixed-threshold erasure code while varying distinct fragment assignment along a second dimension. This ensures that reconstruction is always possible from $F+1$ fragments using the same interpolation method and no additional metadata. Rafture further enables nodes to adapt autonomously based on locally observed information, eliminating the need for global coordination.
  We evaluate Rafture in highly dynamic network settings and show that it simplifies recovery while significantly improving long-term storage consumption under variable network conditions.

</details>


### [65] [eBeeMetrics: An eBPF-based Library Framework for Feedback-free Observability of QoS Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25067)
*Muntaka Ibnath,Mohammadreza Rezvani,Daniel Wong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了eBeeMetrics，一个基于eBPF的库框架，能够仅通过观测系统调用等内核事件来准确推断应用层的QoS指标（如尾延迟和吞吐量），从而解耦系统管理运行时与应用层指标反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的系统管理运行时（如资源管理和电源管理技术）依赖QoS指标（如尾延迟、吞吐量）作为反馈，但这些指标通常无法通过硬件性能计数器观测，也无法在操作系统内核中直接观测。这导致在应用插桩和将QoS指标反馈集成到管理运行时时引入了复杂性和开销。

Method: 提出了eBeeMetrics，这是一个基于eBPF的库框架。它仅利用eBPF可观测的事件（如系统调用）来推导应用层指标。该工具可以作为直接替代方案，将系统管理运行时与QoS指标反馈报告解耦，也可以补充现有的QoS指标以更好地识别服务器端动态。

Result: 在各种延迟敏感的工作负载中，eBeeMetrics推断的指标与现实测量的吞吐量和延迟指标实现了强相关性，证明了其准确性和有效性。该工具已开源。

Conclusion: eBeeMetrics成功填补了系统管理运行时与应用层QoS指标反馈之间的空白，提供了一种低开销、无侵入的方式来实现准确的QoS指标观测，为系统管理决策提供了可靠的反馈支持。

Abstract: Many system management runtimes (SMRs), such as resource management and power management techniques, rely on quality-of-service (QoS) metrics, such as tail latency or throughput, as feedback. These QoS metrics are generally neither observable with hardware performance counters nor directly observable within the OS kernel. This introduces complexity and overhead in instrumenting the application and integrating QoS performance metric feedback with many management runtimes. To bridge this gap, we introduced eBeeMetrics, an eBPF-based library framework to accurately observe application-level metrics derived from only eBPF-observable events, such as system calls. eBeeMetrics can be used as a drop-in replacement to decouple system management runtimes from QoS metric feedback reporting, or can supplement existing QoS metrics to better identify server-side dynamics. eBeeMetrics achieves a strong correlation with real-world measured throughput and latency metrics across various latency-sensitive workloads. The eBeeMetrics tool is open-source; the source code is available at: https://github.com/Ibnathism/eBeeMetrics.

</details>


### [66] [DFLOP: A Data-driven Framework for Multimodal LLM Training Pipeline Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25120)
*Hyeonjun An,Sihyun Kim,Chaerim Lim,Hyunjoon Kim,Rathijit Sen,Sangmin Jung,Hyeonsoo Lee,Dongwook Kim,Takki Yu,Jinkyu Jeong,Youngsok Kim,Kwanghyun Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出DFLOP框架，通过感知数据特征解决多模态大模型分布式训练中的计算倾斜问题，实现最高3.6倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型分布式训练框架是数据盲的，在并行计算时未考虑输入数据特征的差异。异构多模态输入导致不同处理成本，引发阶段和微批次间严重的计算倾斜，造成GPU资源利用不均、同步延迟累积和训练效率下降。

Method: 提出DFLOP，一个数据驱动的多模态LLM训练流水线优化框架。该框架持续分析运行时行为以捕获数据引起的计算差异，并采用预测性调度来平衡各阶段和微批次间的工作负载，将数据特征与执行规划相结合。

Result: 在大规模多模态基准测试上的广泛实验表明，与最先进的分布式训练框架相比，DFLOP实现了高达3.6倍的训练加速，显著提升了GPU利用率和吞吐量。

Conclusion: 通过将数据特征感知纳入分布式训练调度，DFLOP有效解决了多模态大模型训练中的计算倾斜问题，为未来数据感知的训练系统设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable advances by integrating text, image, and audio understanding within a unified architecture. However, existing distributed training frameworks remain fundamentally data-blind: they parallelize computation without accounting for variations in input data characteristics. This data unawareness leads to severe computation skew across stages and microbatches, where heterogeneous multimodal inputs incur different processing costs. Consequently, GPU resources are unevenly utilized, synchronization delays accumulate, and overall training efficiency degrades. To address this limitation, we present DFLOP, a data-driven framework for multimodal LLM training pipeline optimization. DFLOP continuously profiles runtime behavior to capture data-induced computation variance and employs predictive scheduling to balance workloads across stages and microbatches. By coupling data characteristics with execution planning, DFLOP substantially improves GPU utilization and throughput. Extensive experiments on large-scale multimodal benchmarks show that DFLOP achieves up to 3.6x faster training compared to state-of-the-art distributed training frameworks.

</details>


### [67] [Revealing the influence of participant failures on model quality in cross-silo Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25289)
*Fabian Stricker,David Bermbach,Christian Zirpins*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了联邦学习（FL）中因故障导致的参与者缺失对模型性能的影响，发现数据偏斜是关键影响因素，可能导致模型评估过于乐观甚至改变其他因素的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习作为一种分布式系统，在生产环境中容易受到崩溃故障和网络分区等问题的影响，导致参与者缺失，从而威胁学习结果的有效性、稳定性和可重复性。然而，目前尚缺乏关于此类故障对联邦学习结果影响的系统性研究。

Method: 针对图像、表格和时间序列数据开展了广泛实验，系统分析了参与者缺失对模型性能的影响，考虑了数据偏斜、不同可用性模式和模型架构等影响因素，并探讨了场景特定方面，包括全局模型对缺失参与者的效用。

Result: 实验详细揭示了各种影响因素的作用。研究表明，数据偏斜具有强烈影响，往往导致模型评估过于乐观，在某些情况下甚至改变了其他影响因素的效果。

Conclusion: 参与者缺失对联邦学习模型性能有显著影响，其中数据偏斜起着关键作用。这一发现为提高联邦学习系统的可靠性和鲁棒性提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is a paradigm for training machine learning (ML) models in collaborative settings while preserving participants' privacy by keeping raw data local. A key requirement for the use of FL in production is reliability, as insufficient reliability can compromise the validity, stability, and reproducibility of learning outcomes. FL inherently operates as a distributed system and is therefore susceptible to crash failures, network partitioning, and other fault scenarios. Despite this, the impact of such failures on FL outcomes has not yet been studied systematically.
  In this paper, we address this gap by investigating the impact of missing participants in FL. To this end, we conduct extensive experiments on image, tabular, and time-series data and analyze how the absence of participants affects model performance, taking into account influencing factors such as data skewness, different availability patterns, and model architectures. Furthermore, we examine scenario-specific aspects, including the utility of the global model for missing participants. Our experiments provide detailed insights into the effects of various influencing factors. In particular, we show that data skewness has a strong impact, often leading to overly optimistic model evaluations and, in some cases, even altering the effects of other influencing factors.

</details>


### [68] [The Complexity of Distributed Minimum Weight Cycle Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25368)
*Yi-Jun Chang,Yanyu Chen,Dipan Dey,Yonggang Jiang,Gopinath Mishra,Hung Thuan Nguyen,Mingyang Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在CONGEST分布式计算模型下研究了最小权环（MWC）问题，在无向加权图中设计了随机算法实现了近似比与轮复杂度之间的平滑权衡，并证明了匹配的下界，从而对直径较小的图获得了近乎紧确的复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的研究（Manoharan和Ramachandran, PODC 2024）在无向加权图中仅实现了(2+ε)-近似算法且轮复杂度为Õ(n^{2/3}+D)，下界结果为Ω(√n/log n)，上下界之间存在明显差距。本文旨在缩小这一差距，探索近似比与轮复杂度之间的权衡关系。

Method: 在无向加权图中设计了随机算法，通过参数k≥1实现(k+1)-近似，轮复杂度为Õ(n^{(k+1)/(2k+1)} + n^{1/k} + D·n^{1/(4k+2)} + D^{2/5}·n^{2/5+1/(4k+2)})。在下界方面，基于Erdős围长猜想，证明了对于任意整数k≥1，任何(k+1-ε)-近似算法都需要Õ(n^{(k+1)/(2k+1)})轮。

Result: 上界：对于任意实数k≥1，实现了(k+1)-近似算法，轮复杂度提供了近似比与轮复杂度之间的平滑权衡。当k≥2且D=Õ(n^{1/4})时，轮复杂度简化为Õ(n^{(k+1)/(2k+1)})。下界：证明了(k+1-ε)-近似算法需要Õ(n^{(k+1)/(2k+1)})轮，适用于有向无权图和无向加权图，即使图的直径很小（D=Θ(log n)）也成立。

Conclusion: 对于直径较小（D=Õ(n^{1/4})）的图，当k≥2时，上下界在多对数因子内匹配，得到了近乎紧确的分布式复杂度界。这一结果显著改进了先前的工作，填补了MWC问题在CONGEST模型中复杂度认知的空白。

Abstract: We investigate the \emph{minimum weight cycle (MWC)} problem in the $\mathsf{CONGEST}$ model of distributed computing.
  For undirected weighted graphs, we design a randomized algorithm that achieves a $(k+1)$-approximation, for any \emph{real} number $k \ge 1$. The round complexity of algorithm is \[ \tilde{O}\!\Big( n^{\frac{k+1}{2k+1}} + n^{\frac{1}{k}} + D\, n^{\frac{1}{2(2k+1)}} + D^{\frac{2}{5}} n^{\frac{2}{5}+\frac{1}{2(2k+1)}} \Big). \] where $n$ denotes the number of nodes and $D$ is the unweighted diameter of the graph. This result yields a smooth trade-off between approximation ratio and round complexity. In particular, when $k \geq 2$ and $D = \tilde{O}(n^{1/4})$, the bound simplifies to \[ \tilde{O}\!\left(
  n^{\frac{k+1}{2k+1}} \right) \]
  On the lower bound side, assuming the Erdős girth conjecture, we prove that for every \emph{integer} $k \ge 1$, any randomized $(k+1-ε)$-approximation algorithm for MWC requires \[ \tildeΩ\!\left(
  n^{\frac{k+1}{2k+1}} \right) \] rounds. This lower bound holds for both directed unweighted and undirected weighted graphs, and applies even to graphs with small diameter $D = Θ(\log n)$.
  Taken together, our upper and lower bounds \emph{match up to polylogarithmic factors} for graphs of sufficiently small diameter $D = \tilde{O}(n^{1/4})$ (when $k \geq 2$), yielding a nearly tight bound on the distributed complexity of the problem.
  Our results improve upon the previous state of the art: Manoharan and Ramachandran (PODC~2024) demonstrated a $(2+ε)$-approximation algorithm for undirected weighted graphs with round complexity $\tilde{O}(n^{2/3}+D)$, and proved that for any arbitrarily large number $α$, any $α$-approximation algorithm for directed unweighted or undirected weighted graphs requires $Ω(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$ rounds.

</details>


### [69] [PRISM: Dynamic Primitive-Based Forecasting for Large-Scale GPU Cluster Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25378)
*Xin Wu,Fei Teng,Xingwang Li,Bin Zheng,Qiang Duan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了PRISM，一个基于原语的组合预测框架，通过字典驱动的时间分解和自适应频谱细化，有效解决了GPU工作负载预测中的高波动性、多周期性和异构性挑战，在大规模生产追踪数据上达到了最先进的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基础设施中对GPU工作负载进行准确预测对于高效调度、资源分配和功率管理至关重要。然而，现代工作负载具有高波动性、多周期性和异构性特点，传统预测器难以应对这些挑战，因此需要一种新的预测框架来提升预测精度和稳定性。

Method: 提出PRISM框架，采用基于原语的组合预测方法。该框架结合了字典驱动的时间分解和自适应频谱细化技术，通过双重表示方法从多样化的GPU作业中提取稳定且可解释的工作负载特征签名。

Result: 在大规模生产追踪数据集上的评估表明，PRISM达到了最先进的预测结果。该方法显著降低了突发阶段的预测误差，为GPU驱动的AI平台中的动态资源管理提供了稳健且架构感知的基础。

Conclusion: PRISM框架成功解决了GPU工作负载预测中的关键挑战，通过创新的组合预测方法实现了高精度预测，为AI基础设施的动态资源管理提供了可靠的技术支撑，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Accurately forecasting GPU workloads is essential for AI infrastructure, enabling efficient scheduling, resource allocation, and power management. Modern workloads are highly volatile, multiple periodicity, and heterogeneous, making them challenging for traditional predictors. We propose PRISM, a primitive-based compositional forecasting framework combining dictionary-driven temporal decomposition with adaptive spectral refinement. This dual representation extracts stable, interpretable workload signatures across diverse GPU jobs. Evaluated on large-scale production traces, PRISM achieves state-of-the-art results. It significantly reduces burst-phase errors, providing a robust, architecture-aware foundation for dynamic resource management in GPU-powered AI platforms.

</details>


### [70] [SHADOW: Seamless Handoff And Zero-Downtime Orchestrated Workload Migration for Stateful Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25484)
*Hai Dinh-Tuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SHADOW框架，通过ShadowPod策略实现Kubernetes中有状态微服务的零停机迁移，相比基准方法将恢复阶段缩短92%、总迁移时间减少77%，并实现零消息丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes中StatefulSet管理工作负载的迁移存在服务停机问题，由于身份约束禁止两个相同序号的Pod同时运行，导致必须进行顺序的停止-重建循环，造成中位数38.5秒的服务停机时间。

Method: 提出SHADOW框架，实现基于消息的状态微服务迁移（MS2M）方法。引入ShadowPod策略，在目标节点上从CRIU检查点镜像创建影子Pod，同时源Pod继续服务；对StatefulSet工作负载，通过ExchangeFence机制执行身份交换流程，重新检查点影子Pod、创建StatefulSet拥有的替换Pod，并排空消息队列。

Result: 在裸机Kubernetes集群上进行280次迁移运行测试（四种配置和七种消息速率10-120msg/s），相比StatefulSet工作负载的顺序基准：恢复阶段缩短高达92%，完全消除服务停机，总迁移时间减少高达77%，所有280次运行均实现零消息丢失。

Conclusion: SHADOW框架成功解决了StatefulSet工作负载迁移中的服务停机问题，实现了零消息丢失的无缝迁移，显著提升了迁移效率。

Abstract: Migrating stateful microservices in Kubernetes requires careful state management because in-memory state is lost when a container restarts. For StatefulSet-managed workloads, the problem is compounded by identity constraints that prohibit two pods with the same ordinal from running simultaneously, forcing a sequential stop-recreate cycle with a median 38.5s of service downtime. This paper presents SHADOW Seamless Handoff And Zero-Downtime Orchestrated Workload Migration, a Kubernetes-native framework that implements the Message-based Stateful Microservice Migration (MS2M) approach as a Kubernetes Operator. SHADOW introduces the ShadowPod strategy, where a shadow pod is created from a CRIU checkpoint image on the target node while the source pod continues serving traffic, allowing concurrent operation during message replay. For StatefulSet workloads, an identity swap procedure with the ExchangeFence mechanism re-checkpoints the shadow pod, creates a StatefulSet-owned replacement, and drains both message queues to guarantee zero message loss during the handoff. An evaluation on a bare-metal Kubernetes cluster with 280 migration runs across four configurations and seven message rates (10--120msg/s) shows that, compared to the sequential baseline on the same StatefulSet workload, the ShadowPod strategy reduces the restore phase by up to 92%, eliminates service downtime entirely, and reduces total migration time by up to 77%, with zero message loss across all 280 runs.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [Scalable Air-to-Ground Wireless Channel Modeling Using Environmental Context and Generative Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24620)
*Jingyi Tian,Lin Cai*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种环境感知的空地无线信道建模方法，结合数字高程模型和地物信息，利用射线追踪和扩散模型实现高效、准确的信道特性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道（LEO）卫星的快速运动导致传播信道快速变化，尤其在低仰角时信号易受地形遮挡和环境影响。现有研究多依赖假设的统计信道分布，忽视了实际地理环境的影响，难以准确刻画信道特性。

Method: 提出环境感知信道建模方法：利用数字高程模型（DEM）和地物覆盖信息，结合射线追踪（RT）判断视距（LOS）与非视距（NLOS）链路及反射路径；综合绕射损耗、植被吸收和大气衰减模型定量刻画信道。针对RT计算量大问题，利用RT样本和环境特征训练可扩展的扩散模型，实现对任意卫星和地面终端位置的信道性能高效预测。

Result: 实验利用蜂窝网络和LEO卫星链路的实测数据对模型进行验证，结果表明所提方法在真实地理环境中能够有效预测信道性能，验证了模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提环境感知信道建模方法能够结合实际地理环境数据，准确且高效地刻画LEO卫星空地无线信道特性，支持实时决策，为实际部署提供有力支撑。

Abstract: The fast motion of Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellites causes the propagation channel to vary rapidly, and its behavior is strongly shaped by the surrounding environment, especially at low elevation angles where signals are highly susceptible to terrain blockage and other environmental effects. Existing studies mostly rely on assumed statistical channel distributions and therefore ignore the influence of the actual geographic environment. In this paper, we propose an environment-aware channel modeling method for air-to-ground wireless links. We leverage real environmental data, including digital elevation models (DEMs) and land cover information, together with ray tracing (RT) to determine whether a link is line-of-sight (LOS) or non-line-of-sight (NLOS) and to identify possible reflection paths of the signal. The resulting obstruction and reflection profiles are then combined with models of diffraction loss, vegetation absorption, and atmospheric attenuation to quantitatively characterize channel behavior in realistic geographic environments. Since RT is computationally intensive, we use RT-generated samples and environmental features to train a scalable diffusion model that can efficiently predict channel performance for arbitrary satellite and ground terminal positions, thereby supporting real-time decision-making. In the experiments, we validate the proposed model with measurement data from both cellular and LEO satellite links, demonstrating its effectiveness in realistic environments.

</details>


### [72] [Dual-Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Handover Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24634)
*Matteo Salvatori,Filippo Vannella,Sebastian Macaluso,Stylianos E. Trevlakis,Carlos Segura Perales,José Suarez-Varela,Alexandros-Apostolos A. Boulogeorgos,Ioannis Arapakis*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于TD3-D-MA（一种结合图神经网络的多智能体强化学习算法）的去中心化切换优化方法，通过在网络对偶图上优化小区个体偏移（CIO）参数，显著提升了蜂窝网络的吞吐量并具备良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝网络中的切换（HO）控制参数（特别是小区个体偏移CIO）传统上依赖基于规则的启发式方法配置。在网络规模下，CIO调优是一个高度耦合的问题：微小的参数变化可能导致移动流在多个邻区之间重定向，且静态规则在非平稳流量和移动性条件下性能严重下降。因此需要一种能够处理复杂耦合关系并适应动态环境变化的智能优化方法。

Method: 作者将切换优化问题建模为网络对偶图上的去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDP）。在该表示中，每个智能体控制一个邻区对的CIO，并观测其局部对偶图邻域聚合的关键性能指标（KPI）。基于此，提出了TD3-D-MA算法，这是TD3算法的离散多智能体强化学习变体，具有一个在对偶图上操作的共享参数图神经网络（GNN）执行器，以及用于训练的区域级双重评论器，从而改善了密集部署中的信用分配问题。该方法在ns-3系统级仿真器中进行评估，配置了真实网络运营商参数。

Result: 在不同流量场景和网络拓扑结构的实验中，TD3-D-MA相比标准切换启发式方法和集中式强化学习基线方法，显著提升了网络吞吐量。同时，该算法在拓扑结构和流量发生变化时表现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过利用CIO的成对结构并建模为对偶图上的Dec-POMDP问题，结合GNN和多智能体强化学习的TD3-D-MA算法能够有效解决切换参数优化的耦合问题，实现了可扩展的去中心化决策，在提升网络性能的同时保证了在动态环境下的泛化鲁棒性。

Abstract: HandOver (HO) control in cellular networks is governed by a set of HO control parameters that are traditionally configured through rule-based heuristics. A key parameter for HO optimization is the Cell Individual Offset (CIO), defined for each pair of neighboring cells and used to bias HO triggering decisions. At network scale, tuning CIOs becomes a tightly coupled problem: small changes can redirect mobility flows across multiple neighbors, and static rules often degrade under non-stationary traffic and mobility. We exploit the pairwise structure of CIOs by formulating HO optimization as a Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (Dec-POMDP) on the network's dual graph. In this representation, each agent controls a neighbor-pair CIO and observes Key Performance Indicators (KPIs) aggregated over its local dual-graph neighborhood, enabling scalable decentralized decisions while preserving graph locality. Building on this formulation, we propose TD3-D-MA, a discrete Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) variant of the TD3 algorithm with a shared-parameter Graph Neural Network (GNN) actor operating on the dual graph and region-wise double critics for training, improving credit assignment in dense deployments. We evaluate TD3-D-MA in an ns-3 system-level simulator configured with real-world network operator parameters across heterogeneous traffic regimes and network topologies. Results show that TD3-D-MA improves network throughput over standard HO heuristics and centralized RL baselines, and generalizes robustly under topology and traffic shifts.

</details>


### [73] [Spatio-Temporal Semantic Inference for Resilient 6G HRLLC in the Low-Altitude Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24712)
*Chuan-Chi Lai,Ang-Hsun Tsai,Zhu Han*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出EPIC框架，通过时空语义推理实现主动式协调，在6G网络中实现93.5%的反应延迟降低，并在极端网络抖动和信号静默下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空经济的快速发展要求自治空中代理之间具备高可靠协调能力，但传统6G网络中的反应式通信范式容易受到随机网络抖动和间歇性信号静默的影响，尤其在复杂城市峡谷环境中存在连接性缺口。

Method: 提出具身主动推理协调(EPIC)框架，包含时空语义推理(STSI)算子，通过将过时的对等观测投影到主动信念流形上，实现协调环路与物理信号波动的解耦。

Result: 端到端反应延迟平均降低93.5%，将150ms的物理传输延迟掩盖为确定性的10ms执行心跳；对高达100ms的网络抖动表现出策略免疫；在持续50s的极端信号静默期间，加权覆盖效率(WCE)提升10.5%。

Conclusion: EPIC框架为6G超可靠低延迟通信(HRLLC)提供了确定性的韧性保障，证明主动式推理方法可有效解决自治空中代理协调中的网络不确定性问题。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Low-Altitude Economy (LAE) necessitates highly reliable coordination among autonomous aerial agents (AAAs). Traditional reactive communication paradigms in 6G networks are increasingly susceptible to stochastic network jitter and intermittent signaling silence, especially within complex urban canyon environments. To address this connectivity gap, this paper introduces the Embodied Proactive Inference for Coordination (EPIC) framework, featuring a Spatio-Temporal Semantic Inference (STSI) operator designed to decouple the coordination loop from physical signaling fluctuations. By projecting stale peer observations into a proactive belief manifold, EPIC maintains a deterministic reaction latency regardless of the network state. Extensive simulations demonstrate that EPIC achieves an average 93.5% reduction in end-to-end reaction latency, masking physical transmission delays of 150 ms with a deterministic 10 ms execution heartbeat. Crucially, EPIC exhibits strategic immunity to escalating network jitter up to 100 ms and improves the Weighted Coverage Efficiency (WCE) by 10.5% during extreme signaling silence lasting up to 50 s. These results provide the deterministic resilience essential for 6G Hyper-Reliable and Low-Latency Communication (HRLLC).

</details>


### [74] [Quantum Inspired Vehicular Network Optimization for Intelligent Decision Making in Smart Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24971)
*Kamran Ahmad Awan,Sonia Khan,Eman Abdullah Aldakheel,Saif Al-Kuwari,Ahmed Farouk*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出QIVNOM框架，在经典边-云硬件上联合优化车联网通信与城市交通控制，在仿真中实现端到端延迟降低约20%，可靠性提升至96.7%，有效应对网络中断与事故场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网联自动驾驶车辆需要在严格的延迟和可靠性约束下实现城市级协调。然而，现有方法大多将通信与移动性优化分离处理，导致在网络中断和计算资源竞争时性能下降。因此，需要一种能够联合优化通信与交通控制的解决方案。

Method: 提出QIVNOM量子启发框架，在经典边-云硬件上运行，无需量子处理器。该方法将候选路由-信号方案编码为概率叠加态，采用球面投影梯度与退火采样进行更新，通过纠缠风格正则化项耦合网络与移动性决策，并利用Tchebycheff多目标标量化与可行性投影强制执行延迟和可靠性约束。

Result: 在METR-LA校准的SUMO-OMNeT++/Veins仿真中验证，覆盖5×5公里城市区域，采用IEEE 802.11p和5G NR侧行链路。QIVNOM将平均端到端延迟降至57.3毫秒，较最佳基线降低约20%。事故场景下延迟从79毫秒降至62毫秒（-21.5%），RSU中断场景下从86毫秒降至67毫秒（-22.1%）。数据包投递率达96.7%（+2.3个百分点），RSU中断时可靠性达96.8%对基线94.1%。走廊封闭场景下平均行程时间降至12.8分钟，拥堵率降至33%。

Conclusion: QIVNOM通过联合优化V2V/V2I通信与城市交通控制，在正常及故障场景下均显著降低了延迟、提升了可靠性与交通效率，证明了量子启发方法在经典硬件上解决复杂协同优化问题的有效性。

Abstract: Connected and automated vehicles require city-scale coordination under strict latency and reliability constraints. However, many existing approaches optimize communication and mobility separately, which can degrade performance during network outages and under compute contention. This paper presents QIVNOM, a quantum-inspired framework that jointly optimizes vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) and vehicle-to-infrastructure (V2I) communication together with urban traffic control on classical edge--cloud hardware, without requiring a quantum processor. QIVNOM encodes candidate routing--signal plans as probabilistic superpositions and updates them using sphere-projected gradients with annealed sampling to minimize a regularized objective. An entanglement-style regularizer couples networking and mobility decisions, while Tchebycheff multi-objective scalarization with feasibility projection enforces constraints on latency and reliability.
  The proposed framework is evaluated in METR-LA--calibrated SUMO--OMNeT++/Veins simulations over a $5\times5$~km urban map with IEEE 802.11p and 5G NR sidelink. Results show that QIVNOM reduces mean end-to-end latency to 57.3~ms, approximately $20\%$ lower than the best baseline. Under incident conditions, latency decreases from 79~ms to 62~ms ($-21.5\%$), while under roadside unit (RSU) outages, it decreases from 86~ms to 67~ms ($-22.1\%$). Packet delivery reaches $96.7\%$ (an improvement of $+2.3$ percentage points), and reliability remains $96.7\%$ overall, including $96.8\%$ under RSU outages versus $94.1\%$ for the baseline. In corridor-closure scenarios, travel performance also improves, with average travel time reduced to 12.8~min and congestion lowered to $33\%$, compared with 14.5~min and $37\%$ for the baseline.

</details>


### [75] [A Wireless World Model for AI-Native 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25216)
*Ziqi Chen,Yi Ren,Yixuan Huang,Qi Sun,Nan Li,Yuhong Huang,Chih-Lin I,Yifan Li,Liang Xia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了无线世界模型（WWM），这是一个多模态基础框架，通过内化3D几何与信号动态之间的因果关系来预测无线信道的时空演变，在多项下游任务中显著优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的数据驱动方法由于缺乏对电磁波传播的内在理解，难以在动态环境中实现泛化，无法满足将AI集成到6G网络物理层的需求。

Method: 提出了无线世界模型（WWM），采用联合嵌入预测架构和多模态混合专家Transformer，在大规模射线追踪多模态数据集上进行预训练。该模型将信道状态信息、3D点云和用户轨迹融合为统一表征。

Result: WWM在已知环境、未知泛化场景以及真实世界测量数据中，针对五项关键下游任务均取得了卓越性能，持续优于最先进的单模态基础模型和特定任务模型。

Conclusion: WWM成功克服了数据真实性问题，为构建能够适应物理世界的物理感知型6G智能奠定了基础。

Abstract: Integrating AI into the physical layer is a cornerstone of 6G networks. However, current data-driven approaches struggle to generalize across dynamic environments because they lack an intrinsic understanding of electromagnetic wave propagation. We introduce the Wireless World Model (WWM), a multi-modal foundation framework predicting the spatiotemporal evolution of wireless channels by internalizing the causal relationship between 3D geometry and signal dynamics. Pre-trained on a massive ray-traced multi-modal dataset, WWM overcomes the data authenticity gap, further validated under real-world measurement data. Using a joint-embedding predictive architecture with a multi-modal mixture-of-experts Transformer, WWM fuses channel state information, 3D point clouds, and user trajectories into a unified representation. Across the five key downstream tasks supported by WWM, it achieves remarkable performance in seen environments, unseen generalization scenarios, and real-world measurements, consistently outperforming SOTA uni-modal foundation models and task-specific models. This paves the way for physics-aware 6G intelligence that adapts to the physical world.

</details>


### [76] [Lightweight GenAI for Network Traffic Synthesis: Fidelity, Augmentation, and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25507)
*Giampaolo Bovenzi,Domenico Ciuonzo,Jonatan Krolikowski,Antonio Montieri,Alfredo Nascita,Antonio Pescapè,Dario Rossi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用轻量级生成式AI架构（包括Transformer、状态空间和扩散模型）解决网络流量生成任务，在低数据场景下将流量分类性能提升高达40%，其中Transformer模型在保真度和效率之间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的网络流量分类（NTC）日益受限于有限的标注数据和严格的隐私要求。虽然网络流量生成（NTG）能有效缓解数据稀缺问题，但传统生成方法难以建模现代流量的复杂时间动态特性，且通常计算成本高昂。

Method: 采用轻量级生成式AI架构解决NTG任务，包括基于Transformer、状态空间和扩散模型，均针对实际部署进行了优化设计。研究从四个维度进行系统评估：(i)合成流量保真度，(ii)仅合成数据训练，(iii)低数据环境下的数据增强，(iv)计算效率。

Result: 在两个异构数据集上的实验表明，轻量级GenAI模型能保留静态和时间流量特性，其中Transformer和状态空间模型在所有保真度指标上紧密匹配真实分布；仅用合成流量训练的分类器在真实数据上达到高达87%的F1分数；在低数据设置下，GenAI驱动的数据增强将NTC性能提升高达+40%，显著缩小了与全数据训练的差距。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的模型在保真度和效率之间提供了最佳权衡，能够以适度的计算开销实现高质量、隐私感知的流量合成，为实际部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate Network Traffic Classification (NTC) is increasingly constrained by limited labeled data and strict privacy requirements. While Network Traffic Generation (NTG) provides an effective means to mitigate data scarcity, conventional generative methods struggle to model the complex temporal dynamics of modern traffic or/and often incur significant computational cost. In this article, we address the NTG task using lightweight Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) architectures, including transformer-based, state-space, and diffusion models designed for practical deployment. We conduct a systematic evaluation along four axes: (i) (synthetic) traffic fidelity, (ii) synthetic-only training, (iii) data augmentation under low-data regimes, and (iv) computational efficiency. Experiments on two heterogeneous datasets show that lightweight GenAI models preserve both static and temporal traffic characteristics, with transformer and state-space models closely matching real distributions across a complete set of fidelity metrics. Classifiers trained solely on synthetic traffic achieve up to 87% F1-score on real data. In low-data settings, GenAI-driven augmentation improves NTC performance by up to +40%, substantially reducing the gap with full-data training. Overall, transformer-based models provide the best trade-off between fidelity and efficiency, enabling high-quality, privacy-aware traffic synthesis with modest computational overhead.

</details>


### [77] [Cooperative Deep Reinforcement Learning for Fair RIS Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25572)
*Martin Mark Zan,Stefan Schwarz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于协同多智能体强化学习的公平感知RIS资源拍卖机制，有效解决了多小区网络中RIS基础设施的动态分配问题，在保证整体吞吐量的同时显著改善了弱势用户的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多小区无线网络中部署可重构智能表面（RIS）带来了新的资源分配挑战，特别是当各基站的用户负载不均衡时，如何将RIS作为共享基础设施在竞争的基站间进行动态分配成为一个关键问题。现有方法难以平衡小区间的性能差异，需要一种能够兼顾效率和公平性的资源分配机制。

Method: 采用同步升价拍卖机制进行RIS资源分配，并提出一种公平感知的协同多智能体强化学习方法。基站根据预期效用增益和相对服务质量调整竞价策略，同时将中心化计算的性能相关公平性指标纳入智能体的观测中，实现基站间的隐式协调而无需直接通信。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的框架能够有效地将RIS资源重新分配给性能较差的小区，在保持整体吞吐量的同时，显著提升了最差服务用户的速率。公平导向的RIS分配可以通过协同学习实现。

Conclusion: 该方法为未来无线网络中平衡效率与公平性提供了一种灵活工具，证明了通过协同学习实现公平导向的RIS资源分配的可行性，为共享基础设施的资源管理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: The deployment of reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) introduces new challenges for resource allocation in multi-cell wireless networks, particularly when user loads are uneven across base stations. In this work, we consider RISs as shared infrastructure that must be dynamically assigned among competing base stations, and we address this problem using a simultaneous ascending auction mechanism. To mitigate performance imbalances between cells, we propose a fairness-aware collaborative multi-agent reinforcement learning approach in which base stations adapt their bidding strategies based on both expected utility gains and relative service quality. A centrally computed performance-dependent fairness indicator is incorporated into the agents' observations, enabling implicit coordination without direct inter-base-station communication. Simulation results show that the proposed framework effectively redistributes RIS resources toward weaker-performing cells, substantially improving the rates of the worst-served users while preserving overall throughput. The results demonstrate that fairness-oriented RIS allocation can be achieved through cooperative learning, providing a flexible tool for balancing efficiency and equity in future wireless networks.

</details>

### [78] [Experimental Analysis of FreeRTOS Dependability through Targeted Fault Injection Campaigns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25666)
*Luca Mannella,Stefano Di Carlo,Alessandro Savino*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出了KRONOS框架，通过软件故障注入评估FreeRTOS在辐射环境下的可靠性，发现指针和调度器变量损坏常导致崩溃，而多数TCB字段对可用性影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时操作系统（RTOS）在安全关键领域中至关重要，需要确定性和可预测的任务执行。然而，它们越来越多地暴露于电离辐射环境中，这可能损害系统的可靠性，因此需要评估FreeRTOS在此类条件下的表现。

Method: 引入KRONOS，一种基于软件、非侵入式的传播后故障注入（FI）框架，无需专用硬件或调试接口即可向操作系统可见的内核数据结构注入瞬态和永久性故障。使用该框架对FreeRTOS内核核心组件（包括调度器相关变量和任务控制块TCB）进行广泛的故障注入实验，表征内核级损坏对功能正确性、时序行为和可用性的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，指针和关键调度器相关变量的损坏频繁导致系统崩溃，而许多TCB字段对系统可用性的影响较为有限。

Conclusion: KRONOS框架能够有效评估RTOS内核在辐射引起的故障下的脆弱性，为系统设计者提供了关于哪些内核结构更需要保护的重要见解，有助于提升安全关键系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Real-Time Operating Systems (RTOSes) play a crucial role in safety-critical domains, where deterministic and predictable task execution is essential. Yet they are increasingly exposed to ionizing radiation, which can compromise system dependability.
  To assess FreeRTOS under such conditions, we introduce KRONOS, a software-based, non-intrusive post-propagation Fault Injection (FI) framework that injects transient and permanent faults into Operating System-visible kernel data structures without specialized hardware or debug interfaces. Using KRONOS, we conduct an extensive FI campaign on core FreeRTOS kernel components, including scheduler-related variables and Task Control Blocks (TCBs), characterizing the impact of kernel-level corruptions on functional correctness, timing behavior, and availability.
  The results show that corruption of pointer and key scheduler-related variables frequently leads to crashes, whereas many TCB fields have only a limited impact on system availability.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [79] [DyMRL: Dynamic Multispace Representation Learning for Multimodal Event Forecasting in Knowledge Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24636)
*Feng Zhao,Kangzheng Liu,Teng Peng,Yu Yang,Guandong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DyMRL方法，通过多空间表示学习和双重融合演化注意力机制解决动态多模态知识获取与融合问题，在事件预测任务上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注静态设置，忽视了多模态知识的动态获取与融合。在知识获取层面，现有动态学习方法难以捕获深层的感知几何特征；在知识融合层面，基于静态协同注意力的方法无法捕获不同模态对未来事件的历史贡献变化。

Method: 提出DyMRL（动态多空间表示学习）方法：1）在知识获取层面，将欧氏、双曲和复空间的时序结构特征集成到关系消息传递框架中学习深层表示，利用预训练模型赋予时序敏感的视觉和语言智能；2）在知识融合层面，引入双重融合演化注意力机制，以对称方式在不同时间戳为不同模态分配动态学习权重。

Result: 构建了四个多模态时序知识图谱基准数据集；大量实验表明DyMRL在事件预测性能上优于现有的动态单模态和静态多模态基线方法。

Conclusion: DyMRL有效解决了多模态时序知识的动态获取与融合挑战，通过多空间表示学习反映了人类联想思维、高阶抽象和逻辑推理能力，在事件预测任务中取得了优越性能。

Abstract: Accurate representation of multimodal knowledge is crucial for event forecasting in real-world scenarios. However, existing studies have largely focused on static settings, overlooking the dynamic acquisition and fusion of multimodal knowledge. 1) At the knowledge acquisition level, how to learn time-sensitive information of different modalities, especially the dynamic structural modality. Existing dynamic learning methods are often limited to shallow structures across heterogeneous spaces or simple unispaces, making it difficult to capture deep relation-aware geometric features. 2) At the knowledge fusion level, how to learn evolving multimodal fusion features. Existing knowledge fusion methods based on static coattention struggle to capture the varying historical contributions of different modalities to future events. To this end, we propose DyMRL, a Dynamic Multispace Representation Learning approach to efficiently acquire and fuse multimodal temporal knowledge. 1) For the former issue, DyMRL integrates time-specific structural features from Euclidean, hyperbolic, and complex spaces into a relational message-passing framework to learn deep representations, reflecting human intelligences in associative thinking, high-order abstracting, and logical reasoning. Pretrained models endow DyMRL with time-sensitive visual and linguistic intelligences. 2) For the latter concern, DyMRL incorporates advanced dual fusion-evolution attention mechanisms that assign dynamic learning emphases equally to different modalities at different timestamps in a symmetric manner. To evaluate DyMRL's event forecasting performance through leveraging its learned multimodal temporal knowledge in history, we construct four multimodal temporal knowledge graph benchmarks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DyMRL outperforms state-of-the-art dynamic unimodal and static multimodal baseline methods.

</details>


### [80] [How unconstrained machine-learning models learn physical symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24638)
*Michelangelo Domina,Joseph William Abbott,Paolo Pegolo,Filippo Bigi,Michele Ceriotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套度量指标来量化无约束模型学习到的对称性内容，发现通过数据增强策略，无约束模型能够高精度地学习近似等变行为。基于此分析，提出了一种注入最小归纳偏置的策略，在保持无约束架构高表达性和可扩展性的同时，实现了更好的稳定性和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的物理模拟机器学习模型通常使用受约束的数学形式来严格保证对称性。然而，无约束模型（不遵守旋转对称性）在性能上具有竞争力，且通过简单的数据增强策略能够学习到高精度的近似等变行为。目前缺乏对这类模型学习到的对称性内容进行量化分析的严格框架。

Method: 作者引入了严格的度量指标来测量学习表示中的对称性内容，并评估输出满足等变条件的精度。这些指标被应用于两个基于Transformer的无约束模型（用于原子模拟的图神经网络和用于粒子物理的PointNet风格架构），研究对称性信息如何在架构层间传递以及在训练过程中如何被学习。

Result: 研究发现无约束模型能够通过数据增强策略学习到高精度的近似等变行为。通过对对称性处理过程的分析，建立了一个诊断机器学习模型中谱失效模式的严格框架。证明了通过策略性地注入最小归纳偏置，可以在保证物理保真度的同时，实现更高的稳定性和精度。

Conclusion: 研究表明，完全无约束的模型能够学习到近似等变行为，但通过策略性地注入最小必要的归纳偏置，可以在保持无约束架构高表达性和可扩展性优势的同时，确保物理保真度，实现稳定性和精度的最佳平衡。这为物理模拟中机器学习模型的设计提供了新的指导原则。

Abstract: The requirement of generating predictions that exactly fulfill the fundamental symmetry of the corresponding physical quantities has profoundly shaped the development of machine-learning models for physical simulations. In many cases, models are built using constrained mathematical forms that ensure that symmetries are enforced exactly. However, unconstrained models that do not obey rotational symmetries are often found to have competitive performance, and to be able to \emph{learn} to a high level of accuracy an approximate equivariant behavior with a simple data augmentation strategy. In this paper, we introduce rigorous metrics to measure the symmetry content of the learned representations in such models, and assess the accuracy by which the outputs fulfill the equivariant condition. We apply these metrics to two unconstrained, transformer-based models operating on decorated point clouds (a graph neural network for atomistic simulations and a PointNet-style architecture for particle physics) to investigate how symmetry information is processed across architectural layers and is learned during training. Based on these insights, we establish a rigorous framework for diagnosing spectral failure modes in ML models. Enabled by this analysis, we demonstrate that one can achieve superior stability and accuracy by strategically injecting the minimum required inductive biases, preserving the high expressivity and scalability of unconstrained architectures while guaranteeing physical fidelity.

</details>


### [81] [Experiential Reflective Learning for Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24639)
*Marc-Antoine Allard,Arnaud Teinturier,Victor Xing,Gautier Viaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了经验反思学习（ERL）框架，通过反思任务轨迹生成可迁移的启发式规则，解决了大语言模型智能体无法利用过往经验的问题，在Gaia2基准上相比ReAct基线提升了7.8%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的自主智能体虽然具备复杂推理和多步问题解决能力，但存在两大局限：难以适应专业化环境，且无法利用过往交互经验，每次面对新任务都从零开始，缺乏经验积累能力。

Method: 提出经验反思学习（ERL）框架：1）通过反思任务轨迹和结果生成启发式规则（heuristics），提取可跨任务迁移的行动经验；2）测试时根据当前任务检索相关启发式规则并注入智能体上下文中指导执行；3）采用选择性检索机制确保相关性。

Result: 在Gaia2基准测试中，ERL相比ReAct基线成功率提升7.8%，任务完成可靠性显著提高，并超越了先前的经验学习方法。消融实验表明：选择性检索至关重要，且启发式规则比少样本轨迹提示提供更具可迁移性的抽象。

Conclusion: 通过反思单次尝试经验来提取可迁移的启发式规则，能够有效实现智能体的自我改进。该方法证明了从经验中学习抽象规则而非简单复用轨迹是实现跨任务知识迁移的有效途径。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled the development of autonomous agents capable of complex reasoning and multi-step problem solving. However, these agents struggle to adapt to specialized environments and do not leverage past interactions, approaching each new task from scratch regardless of their accumulated experience. We introduce Experiential Reflective Learning (ERL), a simple self-improvement framework that enables rapid environment adaptation through experiential learning. ERL reflects on task trajectories and outcomes to generate heuristics, capturing actionable lessons that transfer across tasks. At test time, relevant heuristics are retrieved based on the current task and injected into the agent's context to guide execution. On the Gaia2 benchmark, ERL improves success rate by 7.8% over a ReAct baseline, with large gains in task completion reliability, and outperforms prior experiential learning methods. Through systematic ablations, we find that selective retrieval is essential and that heuristics provide more transferable abstractions than few-shot trajectory prompting. These results demonstrate that reflecting on single-attempt experiences to extract transferable heuristics enables effective agent self-improvement.

</details>


### [82] [Learning Mesh-Free Discrete Differential Operators with Self-Supervised Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24641)
*Lucas Gerken Starepravo,Georgios Fourtakas,Steven Lind,Ajay B. Harish,Tianning Tang,Jack R. C. King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图神经网络的参数化框架，用于学习无网格离散微分算子，在中等精度区域实现了精度与计算成本的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的无网格离散微分算子存在两难困境：要么计算成本低但精度有限，要么精度高但模板计算成本巨大。现有的方法难以在计算效率和数值精度之间取得理想的平衡。

Method: 利用图神经网络构建参数化框架，通过截断泰勒展开导出的多项式矩约束进行训练。模型直接将局部模板相对位置映射到离散算子权重，从而学习具有多项式一致性的离散微分算子。

Result: 所学习的算子仅依赖局部几何特性，具有分辨率无关性，可在不同粒子配置和控制方程间复用。在标准数值分析诊断测试中，精度优于光滑粒子流体动力学(SPH)方法，在中等精度区域相比代表性高阶一致无网格方法具有更有利的精度-成本权衡。成功应用于求解弱可压缩Navier-Stokes方程。

Conclusion: 神经网络能够学习经典多项式一致性，同时保持对不规则邻域几何的鲁棒性，为无网格方法中精度与效率的权衡问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Mesh-free numerical methods provide flexible discretisations for complex geometries; however, classical meshless discrete differential operators typically trade low computational cost for limited accuracy or high accuracy for substantial per-stencil computation. We introduce a parametrised framework for learning mesh-free discrete differential operators using a graph neural network trained via polynomial moment constraints derived from truncated Taylor expansions. The model maps local stencils relative positions directly to discrete operator weights. The current work demonstrates that neural networks can learn classical polynomial consistency while retaining robustness to irregular neighbourhood geometry. The learned operators depend only on local geometry, are resolution-agnostic, and can be reused across particle configurations and governing equations. We evaluate the framework using standard numerical analysis diagnostics, showing improved accuracy over Smoothed Particle Hydrodynamics, and a favourable accuracy-cost trade-off relative to a representative high-order consistent mesh-free method in the moderate-accuracy regime. Applicability is demonstrated by solving the weakly compressible Navier-Stokes equations using the learned operators.

</details>


### [83] [Physics-Informed Neural Network Digital Twin for Dynamic Tray-Wise Modeling of Distillation Columns under Transient Operating Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24644)
*Debadutta Patra,Ayush Bardhan Tripathy,Soumya Ranjan Sahu,Sucheta Panda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息神经网络(PINN)的精馏塔数字孪生框架，通过将热力学约束嵌入神经网络损失函数，实现了对二元精馏塔动态过程的逐塔盘精确建模，其预测精度相比纯数据驱动方法提高了44.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生技术与物理信息机器学习结合具有变革工业过程监控和优化的潜力。现有纯数据驱动方法难以满足热力学约束，导致物理不可信的预测结果。因此需要开发能够融合物理知识与数据的模型，实现对精馏塔等复杂工业过程的准确、可靠建模。

Method: 提出PINN数字孪生框架，将汽液平衡（修正拉乌尔定律）、塔盘级物料与能量平衡、McCabe-Thiele图解法等基本热力学约束通过物理残差项直接嵌入神经网络损失函数。采用自适应损失权重方案平衡数据保真度与物理一致性目标。使用Aspen HYSYS生成的961个时间戳高保真合成数据集进行训练，覆盖8小时瞬态操作和16个传感器流。

Result: 在HX摩尔分数预测上，PINN模型实现RMSE为0.00143，R²为0.9887，相比最优纯数据基线方法（LSTM、MLP、GRU、Transformer、DeepONet）误差降低44.6%，同时严格满足热力学约束。模型准确捕捉了进料塔盘响应、回流比变化和压力瞬态等塔内动态特性。

Conclusion: 所提出的PINN数字孪生为工业精馏过程的实时软测量、模型预测控制和异常检测奠定了坚实基础，证明了将物理约束嵌入神经网络的有效性，为构建物理可信的工业过程数字孪生提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Digital twin technology, when combined with physics-informed machine learning with simulation results of Aspen, offers transformative capabilities for industrial process monitoring, control, and optimization. In this work, the proposed model presents a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) digital twin framework for the dynamic, tray-wise modeling of binary distillation columns operating under transient conditions. The architecture of the proposed model embeds fundamental thermodynamic constraints, including vapor-liquid equilibrium (VLE) described by modified Raoult's law, tray-level mass and energy balances, and the McCabe-Thiele graphical methodology directly into the neural network loss function via physics residual terms. The model is trained and evaluated on a high-fidelity synthetic dataset of 961 timestamped measurements spanning 8 hours of transient operation, generated in Aspen HYSYS for a binary HX/TX distillation system comprising 16 sensor streams. An adaptive loss-weighting scheme balances the data fidelity and physics consistency objectives during training. Compared to five data-driven baselines (LSTM, vanilla MLP, GRU, Transformer, DeepONet), the proposed PINN achieves an RMSE of 0.00143 for HX mole fraction prediction (R^2 = 0.9887), representing a 44.6% reduction over the best data-only baseline, while strictly satisfying thermodynamic constraints. Tray-wise temperature and composition profiles predicted under transient perturbations demonstrate that the digital twin accurately captures column dynamics including feed tray responses, reflux ratio variations, and pressure transients. These results establish the proposed PINN digital twin as a robust foundation for real-time soft sensing, model-predictive control, and anomaly detection in industrial distillation processes.

</details>


### [84] [A Unified Memory Perspective for Probabilistic Trustworthy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25692)
*Xueji Zhao,Likai Pei,Jianbo Liu,Kai Ni,Ningyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的数据访问视角，将确定性访问视为随机采样的特例，揭示了随机性需求会降低有效数据访问效率并导致系统进入熵限制状态，并据此建立了内存级评估标准来分析传统架构的局限和新兴概率存内计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可信人工智能对鲁棒性、可解释性、安全性和隐私的需求增加，概率计算变得愈发重要。在实际系统中，这类工作负载将确定性数据访问与多次随机采样交织，导致性能瓶颈从算术单元转移到必须同时提供数据和随机性的内存系统。

Method: 提出一种统一的数据访问视角，将确定性访问视为随机采样的极限情况，使两种模式可在共同框架下分析。基于此洞察，定义了内存级评估标准，包括统一操作、分布可编程性、效率、对硬件非理想因素的鲁棒性以及并行兼容性。

Result: 研究表明，增加随机性需求会降低有效的数据访问效率，并可能使系统进入熵限制运行状态。基于定义的评估标准，分析了传统架构的局限性，并审视了新兴的概率存内计算方法，这些方法将采样与内存访问集成。

Conclusion: 本文概述了面向可信AI的可扩展硬件发展路径，指出将采样与内存访问集成的概率存内计算方法是解决传统架构局限、满足日益增长的随机性需求的关键方向。

Abstract: Trustworthy artificial intelligence increasingly relies on probabilistic computation to achieve robustness, interpretability, security and privacy. In practical systems, such workloads interleave deterministic data access with repeated stochastic sampling across models, data paths and system functions, shifting performance bottlenecks from arithmetic units to memory systems that must deliver both data and randomness. Here we present a unified data-access perspective in which deterministic access is treated as a limiting case of stochastic sampling, enabling both modes to be analyzed within a common framework. This view reveals that increasing stochastic demand reduces effective data-access efficiency and can drive systems into entropy-limited operation. Based on this insight, we define memory-level evaluation criteria, including unified operation, distribution programmability, efficiency, robustness to hardware non-idealities and parallel compatibility. Using these criteria, we analyze limitations of conventional architectures and examine emerging probabilistic compute-in-memory approaches that integrate sampling with memory access, outlining pathways toward scalable hardware for trustworthy AI.

</details>


### [85] [Can LLMs Beat Classical Hyperparameter Optimization Algorithms? A Study on autoresearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24647)
*Fabio Ferreira,Lucca Wobbe,Arjun Krishnakumar,Frank Hutter,Arber Zela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个名为autoresearch的超参数优化测试平台，发现经典HPO方法在固定搜索空间下优于LLM代理，但LLM在无约束代码编辑场景能缩小差距；提出的混合方法Centaur结合CMA-ES内部状态与LLM，取得了最佳性能，且仅需0.8B参数模型即可有效工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于系统比较经典超参数优化算法与基于LLM的方法在超参数调优任务中的性能差异，探索LLM代理在无约束搜索空间中通过直接编辑训练代码进行优化的潜力，并解决小中型LLM难以追踪优化状态而经典方法缺乏领域知识的问题。

Method: 方法包括：1）构建autoresearch仓库作为测试平台，允许LLM代理在无约束搜索空间中直接编辑训练代码；2）在固定计算预算和约束下，对比经典HPO算法（CMA-ES、TPE）与LLM方法在小型语言模型超参数调优上的表现；3）提出Centaur混合方法，将CMA-ES的内部状态（均值向量、步长、协方差矩阵）与LLM共享，结合经典优化器的状态追踪能力与LLM的领域知识。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）在固定超参数搜索空间中，CMA-ES和TPE等经典方法持续优于LLM代理；2）在无约束搜索空间中，直接编辑源代码的LLM代理显著缩小了与经典方法的差距，即使仅使用27B参数的开源模型；3）避免内存溢出的方法优于高搜索多样性方法，可靠性比探索广度更重要；4）Centaur混合方法取得最佳结果，其0.8B变体优于27B变体，表明小型LLM配合强经典优化器已足够；5）0.8B模型不足以进行无约束代码编辑，但足以进行混合优化。

Conclusion: 结论指出：经典HPO方法与LLM方法各有优劣——前者缺乏领域知识但善于状态追踪，后者具有领域知识但难以跨试验追踪优化状态；Centaur成功融合两者优势，证明了小型LLM（0.8B）在与强经典优化器结合时即可达到最优性能，为未来HPO算法设计提供了新的混合范式方向。

Abstract: The autoresearch repository enables an LLM agent to search for optimal hyperparameter configurations on an unconstrained search space by editing the training code directly. Given a fixed compute budget and constraints, we use \emph{autoresearch} as a testbed to compare classical hyperparameter optimization (HPO) algorithms against LLM-based methods on tuning the hyperparameters of a small language model. Within a fixed hyperparameter search space, classical HPO methods such as CMA-ES and TPE consistently outperform LLM-based agents. However, an LLM agent that directly edits training source code in an unconstrained search space narrows the gap to classical methods substantially despite using only a self-hosted open-weight 27B model. Methods that avoid out-of-memory failures outperform those with higher search diversity, suggesting that reliability matters more than exploration breadth. While small and mid-sized LLMs struggle to track optimization state across trials, classical methods lack domain knowledge. To bridge this gap, we introduce Centaur, a hybrid that shares CMA-ES's internal state, including mean vector, step-size, and covariance matrix, with an LLM. Centaur achieves the best result in our experiments, with its 0.8B variant outperforming the 27B variant, suggesting that a cheap LLM suffices when paired with a strong classical optimizer. The 0.8B model is insufficient for unconstrained code editing but sufficient for hybrid optimization, while scaling to 27B provides no advantage for fixed search space methods with the open-weight models tested. Code is available at https://github.com/ferreirafabio/autoresearch-automl.

</details>


### [86] [Energy-Efficient Hierarchical Federated Anomaly Detection for the Internet of Underwater Things via Selective Cooperative Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24648)
*Kenechi Omeke,Michael Mollel,Lei Zhang,Qammer H. Abbasi,Muhammad Ali Imran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向水下物联网异常检测的能效分层联邦学习框架，通过三层架构、可行性感知关联、压缩传输和选择性协作机制，在水声通信受限条件下实现了高检测精度与低能耗的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下声学链路存在低带宽、高能耗、通信距离受限等问题，导致标准扁平联邦学习在水下部署时面临远程传输昂贵和传感器参与率低的双重限制，亟需针对水下环境的能效优化学习框架。

Method: 提出三层分层联邦学习框架：（1）可行性感知的传感器-雾节点关联；（2）压缩模型更新传输；（3）雾节点间的选择性协作聚合。架构将大部分通信局限在短距离簇内，仅当小规模簇能从邻近大规模簇受益时才激活雾间协作，并采用物理水声信道模型进行联合评估。

Result: 在200节点合成部署中，仅约48%的传感器可直接到达网关，而分层学习通过可行雾路径实现全部参与。选择性协作匹配了持续雾间交换的检测精度，同时能耗降低31-33%；压缩上传在匹配灵敏度测试中总能耗降低71-95%。三个真实基准实验表明低开销分层方法在检测质量上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了水下声学通信约束下的联邦学习难题，在保持高检测性能的同时显著降低能耗并确保全网参与，为受限水下环境提供了实用的设计指导。

Abstract: Anomaly detection is a core service in the Internet of Underwater Things, yet training accurate distributed models underwater is difficult because acoustic links are low-bandwidth, energy-intensive, and often unable to support direct sensor-to-surface communication. Standard flat federated learning therefore faces two coupled limitations in underwater deployments: expensive long-range transmissions and reduced participation when only a subset of sensors can reach the gateway. This paper proposes an energy-efficient hierarchical federated learning framework for underwater anomaly detection based on three components: feasibility-aware sensor-to-fog association, compressed model-update transmission, and selective cooperative aggregation among fog nodes. The proposed three-tier architecture localises most communication within short-range clusters while activating fog-to-fog exchange only when smaller clusters can benefit from nearby larger neighbours. A physics-grounded underwater acoustic model is used to evaluate detection quality, communication energy, and network participation jointly. In large synthetic deployments, only about 48% of sensors can directly reach the gateway in the 200-sensor case, whereas hierarchical learning preserves full participation through feasible fog paths. Selective cooperation matches the detection accuracy of always-on inter-fog exchange while reducing its energy by 31-33%, and compressed uploads reduce total energy by 71-95% in matched sensitivity tests. Experiments on three real benchmarks further show that low-overhead hierarchical methods remain competitive in detection quality, while flat federated learning defines the minimum-energy operating point. These results provide practical design guidance for underwater deployments operating under severe acoustic communication constraints.

</details>


### [87] [Amplified Patch-Level Differential Privacy for Free via Random Cropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24695)
*Kaan Durmaz,Jan Schuchardt,Sebastian Schmidt,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了随机裁剪这一常见数据增强技术在差分隐私训练中的额外价值：当敏感内容空间局部化时，随机裁剪能以一定概率排除敏感信息，从而作为第三种随机性来源放大差分隐私，在不改变模型架构或训练流程的情况下提升隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算机视觉中，随机裁剪是最常用的数据增强技术之一，但其在差分隐私机器学习模型训练中的作用尚未被探索。研究者注意到当图像中的敏感内容（如人脸或车牌）在空间上局部化时，随机裁剪可以概率性地将这些内容排除在模型输入之外，这可能成为差分隐私训练中的额外随机性来源。

Method: 研究团队引入了视觉数据的块级相邻关系（patch-level neighboring relation），并推导了差分隐私随机梯度下降（DP-SGD）结合随机裁剪时的紧致隐私边界。他们量化了块包含概率（patch inclusion probability），并展示了其如何与小批量采样组合从而产生更低的有效采样率。通过理论分析，形式化了随机裁剪作为第三种随机性来源对差分隐私的放大效应。

Result: 实证验证表明，块级放大效应在多个分割架构和数据集上都能改善隐私-效用权衡。研究证明，将隐私核算与领域结构以及现有的额外随机性来源对齐，可以在不增加额外成本的情况下获得更强的隐私保证。随机裁剪引入的额外随机性与梯度噪声、小批量采样共同作用，增强了差分隐私保护。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了数据增强技术中固有的随机性可以被系统地利用来增强差分隐私保证，而无需修改模型架构或训练程序。这一发现为计算机视觉领域的隐私保护机器学习提供了新的视角：通过对齐隐私核算与领域结构和现有随机性来源，可以以零额外成本获得更强的隐私保证。

Abstract: Random cropping is one of the most common data augmentation techniques in computer vision, yet the role of its inherent randomness in training differentially private machine learning models has thus far gone unexplored. We observe that when sensitive content in an image is spatially localized, such as a face or license plate, random cropping can probabilistically exclude that content from the model's input. This introduces a third source of stochasticity in differentially private training with stochastic gradient descent, in addition to gradient noise and minibatch sampling. This additional randomness amplifies differential privacy without requiring changes to model architecture or training procedure. We formalize this effect by introducing a patch-level neighboring relation for vision data and deriving tight privacy bounds for differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) when combined with random cropping. Our analysis quantifies the patch inclusion probability and shows how it composes with minibatch sampling to yield a lower effective sampling rate. Empirically, we validate that patch-level amplification improves the privacy-utility trade-off across multiple segmentation architectures and datasets. Our results demonstrate that aligning privacy accounting with domain structure and additional existing sources of randomness can yield stronger guarantees at no additional cost.

</details>


### [88] [Training LLMs for Multi-Step Tool Orchestration with Constrained Data Synthesis and Graduated Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24709)
*Cheng Jiayang,Xin Liu,Zhihan Zhang,Haoyang Wen,Zixuan Zhang,Qingyu Yin,Shiyang Li,Priyanka Nigam,Bing Yin,Chao Zhang,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个强化学习框架，通过构建大规模真实API响应缓存环境来合成多步工具编排数据，并设计了分级奖励机制（原子有效性+编排正确性），显著提升了大语言模型在多步工具调用任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多步工具编排任务中，最先进模型在完整序列执行上经常失败，参数值错误占很大比例；现有训练环境仅关注简单单轮函数调用和模拟数据，且二值奖励无法为部分正确性提供信号。

Method: 构建基于大规模真实API响应缓存的强化学习环境，实现可控复杂度的多步编排轨迹数据合成流水线；提出分级奖励设计，将正确性分解为原子有效性（细粒度单个函数调用正确性）和编排正确性（工具序列及依赖关系正确性）。

Result: 在ComplexFuncBench上，该方法在轮次准确率方面取得了显著提升；消融研究表明两个奖励组件都至关重要，单独使用任一组件都会显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了多步工具编排的训练数据和环境问题，分级奖励设计为模型学习提供了更细粒度的反馈信号，两种奖励组件缺一不可，为复杂工具编排任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-step tool orchestration, where LLMs must invoke multiple dependent APIs in the correct order while propagating intermediate outputs, remains challenging. State-of-the-art models frequently fail on full sequence execution, with parameter value errors accounting for a significant portion of failures. Training models to handle such workflows faces two obstacles: existing environments focus on simple per-turn function calls with simulated data, and binary rewards provide no signal for partial correctness.
  We present a framework addressing both challenges. First, we construct a reinforcement learning environment backed by a large-scale cache of real API responses, enabling a data synthesis pipeline that samples valid multi-step orchestration traces with controllable complexity and significantly higher generation efficiency than unconstrained methods. Second, we propose a graduated reward design that decomposes correctness into atomic validity (individual function call correctness at increasing granularity) and orchestration (correct tool sequencing with dependency respect). On ComplexFuncBench, our approach demonstrates substantial improvements in turn accuracy. Ablation studies confirm both reward components are essential: using either alone significantly degrades performance.

</details>


### [89] [Can an Actor-Critic Optimization Framework Improve Analog Design Optimization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24714)
*Sounak Dutta,Fin Amin,Sushil Panda,Jonathan Rabe,Yuejiang Wen,Paul Franzon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个演员-评论家优化框架（ACOF），用于模拟电路尺寸设计，通过将优化过程分为提案和评估两个角色，在保持与标准仿真流程兼容的同时，使搜索过程更稳健、可解释。实验结果表明，相比最强基准，ACOF在top-10品质因数上平均提升38.9%，遗憾值平均降低24.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟电路设计常因器件尺寸或偏置的微小变化需要昂贵的仿真周期而变得缓慢，高质量解决方案通常仅占据巨大搜索空间中的狭窄部分。现有优化器虽然减轻了部分负担，但缺乏设计师在决定下一步搜索位置时使用的判断力。

Method: 提出演员-评论家优化框架（ACOF），将优化过程分解为提案和评估两个角色：演员负责建议设计空间中有潜力的区域，评论家审查这些选择、执行设计合法性检查，并在进展受阻时重新定向搜索。这种结构在保持与标准仿真流程兼容的同时，使搜索过程更具目的性、稳定性和可解释性。

Result: 在测试电路上，ACOF相比最强竞争基准，top-10品质因数平均提升38.9%，遗憾值平均降低24.7%。在单个电路上，品质因数峰值提升达70.5%，遗憾值降低42.2%。

Conclusion: 通过将迭代推理与仿真驱动的搜索相结合，ACOF框架为跨挑战性设计空间的自动化模拟电路尺寸设计提供了一条更透明的路径。

Abstract: Analog design often slows down because even small changes to device sizes or biases require expensive simulation cycles, and high-quality solutions typically occupy only a narrow part of a very large search space. While existing optimizers reduce some of this burden, they largely operate without the kind of judgment designers use when deciding where to search next. This paper presents an actor-critic optimization framework (ACOF) for analog sizing that brings that form of guidance into the loop. Rather than treating optimization as a purely black-box search problem, ACOF separates the roles of proposal and evaluation: an actor suggests promising regions of the design space, while a critic reviews those choices, enforces design legality, and redirects the search when progress is hampered. This structure preserves compatibility with standard simulator-based flows while making the search process more deliberate, stable, and interpretable. Across our test circuits, ACOF improves the top-10 figure of merit by an average of 38.9% over the strongest competing baseline and reduces regret by an average of 24.7%, with peak gains of 70.5% in FoM and 42.2% lower regret on individual circuits. By combining iterative reasoning with simulation-driven search, the framework offers a more transparent path toward automated analog sizing across challenging design spaces.

</details>


### [90] [Contrastive Learning Boosts Deterministic and Generative Models for Weather Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24744)
*Nathan Bailey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPARTA方法，利用对比学习处理稀疏天气数据，通过创新的数据增强、损失函数和物理知识融合技术，证明了对比学习在稀疏地学数据压缩中的可行性和优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气数据具有高维度和多模态特性，需要压缩为低维嵌入以提高下游任务效率。现有对比学习在天气数据上的研究未充分比较其与自编码器等方法的优劣，且未考虑现实世界常见的稀疏数据问题。亟需探索对比学习如何为稀疏天气数据创建鲁棒嵌入。

Method: 提出SPARTA框架：1)通过对比损失项对齐稀疏样本与完整样本；2)引入时间感知的批次采样策略；3)设计循环一致性损失改善潜在空间结构；4)提出新型图神经网络融合技术注入物理领域知识。

Result: 在ERA5数据集上的广泛实验表明，对比学习是稀疏地学数据的可行且有利的压缩方法，在下游任务中表现出性能提升。

Conclusion: 对比学习能够有效处理稀疏天气数据，生成更鲁棒的低维嵌入，为下游预测和极端天气检测等任务提供支持，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Weather data, comprising multiple variables, poses significant challenges due to its high dimensionality and multimodal nature. Creating low-dimensional embeddings requires compressing this data into a compact, shared latent space. This compression is required to improve the efficiency and performance of downstream tasks, such as forecasting or extreme-weather detection.
  Self-supervised learning, particularly contrastive learning, offers a way to generate low-dimensional, robust embeddings from unlabelled data, enabling downstream tasks when labelled data is scarce. Despite initial exploration of contrastive learning in weather data, particularly with the ERA5 dataset, the current literature does not extensively examine its benefits relative to alternative compression methods, notably autoencoders. Moreover, current work on contrastive learning does not investigate how these models can incorporate sparse data, which is more common in real-world data collection. It is critical to explore and understand how contrastive learning contributes to creating more robust embeddings for sparse weather data, thereby improving performance on downstream tasks.
  Our work extensively explores contrastive learning on the ERA5 dataset, aligning sparse samples with complete ones via a contrastive loss term to create SPARse-data augmented conTRAstive spatiotemporal embeddings (SPARTA). We introduce a temporally aware batch sampling strategy and a cycle-consistency loss to improve the structure of the latent space. Furthermore, we propose a novel graph neural network fusion technique to inject domain-specific physical knowledge. Ultimately, our results demonstrate that contrastive learning is a feasible and advantageous compression method for sparse geoscience data, thereby enhancing performance in downstream tasks.

</details>


### [91] [Grokking as a Falsifiable Finite-Size Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24746)
*Yuda Bi,Chenyu Zhang,Qiheng Wang,Vince D Calhoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为“顿悟（grokking）”现象的相变描述提供了可证伪的有限尺寸验证方法，通过有限尺寸标度分析支持了相变解释而非平滑过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “顿悟”现象（即早期记忆化后延迟泛化的出现）常被用相变语言描述，但该主张缺乏可证伪的有限尺寸输入，导致这一类比缺乏严格的定量验证基础。

Method: 将群\mathbb{Z}_p的阶p视为可容许广延变量，并将留出的谱头尾对比作为表示级序参量，采用凝聚态物理风格的诊断链：对粗网格扫描和近临界密集审计，使用类Binder交叉分析有限尺寸边界，并通过磁化率比较进行模型选择。

Result: Binder类交叉揭示了共享的有限尺寸边界；在近临界审计中，磁化率比较强烈不支持平滑过渡解释（ΔAIC=16.8），即相变解释更优。

Conclusion: 顿悟中的相变语言现在可以作为定量的有限尺寸主张进行检验，而不仅仅作为类比使用；不过目前仍无法确定相变的阶数。

Abstract: Grokking -- the delayed onset of generalization after early memorization -- is often described with phase-transition language, but that claim has lacked falsifiable finite-size inputs. Here we supply those inputs by treating the group order $p$ of $\mathbb{Z}_p$ as an admissible extensive variable and a held-out spectral head-tail contrast as a representation-level order parameter, then apply a condensed-matter-style diagnostic chain to coarse-grid sweeps and a dense near-critical addition audit. Binder-like crossings reveal a shared finite-size boundary, and susceptibility comparison strongly disfavors a smooth-crossover interpretation ($Δ\mathrm{AIC}=16.8$ in the near-critical audit). Phase-transition language in grokking can therefore be tested as a quantitative finite-size claim rather than invoked as analogy alone, although the transition order remains unresolved at present.

</details>


### [92] [Light Cones For Vision: Simple Causal Priors For Visual Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24753)
*Manglam Kartik,Neel Tushar Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Worldline Slot Attention方法，将对象建模为时空世界线轨迹；欧氏世界线表现极差，而洛伦兹世界线实现了约6倍的性能提升，证明了视觉层次发现需要编码非对称因果性的几何结构（洛伦兹光锥）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准视觉模型将对象视为欧氏空间中的独立点，无法捕捉“部分-整体”等层次结构，且缺乏对因果性的编码能力。

Method: 引入Worldline Slot Attention架构，将对象建模为穿越时空世界线的持久轨迹，每个对象在不同层次上有共享空间位置但时间坐标不同的多个槽；引入洛伦兹几何结构作为归纳偏置。

Result: 欧氏世界线准确率仅为0.078（低于随机基线0.33）；洛伦兹世界线在三个数据集上达到0.479-0.661（约6倍提升）；模型仅用11K参数，并超越双曲嵌入基线。

Conclusion: 层次化对象发现需要编码非对称因果性的几何结构；双曲嵌入的树结构不如洛伦兹光锥的因果结构有效，后者是处理视觉层次的自然归纳偏置。

Abstract: Standard vision models treat objects as independent points in Euclidean space, unable to capture hierarchical structure like parts within wholes. We introduce Worldline Slot Attention, which models objects as persistent trajectories through spacetime worldlines, where each object has multiple slots at different hierarchy levels sharing the same spatial position but differing in temporal coordinates. This architecture consistently fails without geometric structure: Euclidean worldlines achieve 0.078 level accuracy, below random chance (0.33), while Lorentzian worldlines achieve 0.479-0.661 across three datasets: a 6x improvement replicated over 20+ independent runs. Lorentzian geometry also outperforms hyperbolic embeddings showing visual hierarchies require causal structure (temporal dependency) rather than tree structure (radial branching). Our results demonstrate that hierarchical object discovery requires geometric structure encoding asymmetric causality, an inductive bias absent from Euclidean space but natural to Lorentzian light cones, achieved with only 11K parameters. The code is available at: https://github.com/iclrsubmissiongram/loco.

</details>


### [93] [Transformers in the Dark: Navigating Unknown Search Spaces via Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24780)
*Jungtaek Kim,Thomas Zeng,Ziqian Lin,Minjae Lee,Chungpa Lee,Jy-yong Sohn,Hyung Il Koo,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究表明，Transformer架构本身具备足够的表达能力来近似树搜索算法，无需外部搜索组件，且通过任务导向的微调可以充分激发预训练大语言模型的搜索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然将大语言模型与外部搜索算法结合能提升问题求解效果，但引入外部组件会增加系统复杂度。因此，研究者提出核心问题：大语言模型或其底层Transformer架构能否直接近似搜索算法？

Method: 研究者引入了一个简化框架——"带老虎机反馈的未知树搜索"，其中树扩展和反馈信号由外部指定，用于可控地评估搜索能力。在此框架下，研究者从理论层面分析Transformer的表达能力，并从头训练Transformer模型来近似不同的搜索策略。

Result: Transformer在理论上具备足够表达能力来实现不同的搜索策略，并能从零开始训练以近似这些策略。模型能够泛化到未见过的条件，如更长的时间跨度或更深的树结构。此外，对预训练LLM进行搜索轨迹微调可以解锁其完整的搜索能力。

Conclusion: Transformer架构本身可以作为搜索算法的近似器，无需依赖外部搜索组件。这为简化大语言模型问题求解流程提供了理论基础，同时表明预训练语言模型通过适当的任务导向训练能够获得强大的搜索能力。

Abstract: Effective problem solving with Large Language Models (LLMs) can be enhanced when they are paired with external search algorithms. By viewing the space of diverse ideas and their follow-up possibilities as a tree structure, the search algorithm can navigate such a search space and guide the LLM toward better solutions more efficiently. While the search algorithm enables an effective balance between exploitation and exploration of a tree-structured space, the need for an external component can complicate the overall problem-solving process. We therefore pose the following question: Can LLMs or their underlying Transformer architectures approximate a search algorithm? To answer this question, we first introduce a simplified framework in which tree extensions and feedback signals are externally specified, allowing for controlled evaluation of search capabilities. We call this setting unknown tree search with bandit feedback. Within this setting, we show that Transformers are theoretically expressive enough to implement distinct search strategies and can be trained from scratch to approximate those strategies. Our Transformer models exhibit the possibility of generalizing to unseen conditions such as longer horizons or deeper trees. Furthermore, we demonstrate that continued task-focused training unlocks the complete capabilities of a pretrained LLM, by fine-tuning the LLM on search trajectories.

</details>


### [94] [Local learning for stable backpropagation-free neural network training towards physical learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24790)
*Yaqi Guo,Fabian Braun,Bastiaan Ketelaar,Stephanie Tan,Richard Norte,Siddhant Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FFzero，一种无需反向传播和自动微分的前向-only学习框架，实现了物理神经网络的在片训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播和自动微分推动了深度学习的成功，但芯片制造的物理限制和深度学习不断上升的环境成本促使人们寻找替代学习范式，如物理神经网络。然而，现有大多数物理神经网络在训练上仍然依赖数字计算，主要是因为反向传播和自动微分在物理系统中难以实现。

Method: FFzero框架结合了逐层局部学习、基于原型的表示以及仅通过前向评估实现的基于方向导数的优化。该方法仅依赖前向计算进行训练，完全避免了反向传播和自动微分过程。

Result: 证明了局部学习在前向优化下有效，而反向传播在此情况下失败；FFzero可泛化至多层感知机和卷积神经网络，适用于分类和回归任务；在模拟光子神经网络上的示例演示了其为无反向传播的原位物理学习提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: FFzero为克服物理神经网络训练难题提供了新范式，通过纯前向计算实现稳定训练，为物理系统的在片学习开辟了实用路径。

Abstract: While backpropagation and automatic differentiation have driven deep learning's success, the physical limits of chip manufacturing and rising environmental costs of deep learning motivate alternative learning paradigms such as physical neural networks. However, most existing physical neural networks still rely on digital computing for training, largely because backpropagation and automatic differentiation are difficult to realize in physical systems. We introduce FFzero, a forward-only learning framework enabling stable neural network training without backpropagation or automatic differentiation. FFzero combines layer-wise local learning, prototype-based representations, and directional-derivative-based optimization through forward evaluations only. We show that local learning is effective under forward-only optimization, where backpropagation fails. FFzero generalizes to multilayer perceptron and convolutional neural networks across classification and regression. Using a simulated photonic neural network as an example, we demonstrate that FFzero provides a viable path toward backpropagation-free in-situ physical learning.

</details>


### [95] [A Practical Guide Towards Interpreting Time-Series Deep Clinical Predictive Models: A Reproducibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24828)
*Yongda Fan,John Wu,Andrea Fitzpatrick,Naveen Baskaran,Jimeng Sun,Adam Cross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个综合基准，用于评估多种可解释性方法在不同临床预测任务和模型架构上的表现。研究发现，合理使用的注意力机制是高效且可靠的可解释方法，而KernelSHAP和LIME等黑盒解释器在时间序列临床任务中计算不可行，部分方法则因不稳定而不可信。作者基于发现提出了改进临床预测模型可解释性的指南，并已通过PyHealth开源框架发布代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策具有高风险性，必须具备明确理由，因此模型可解释性对于在深度临床模型部署前进行审计至关重要。随着模型架构和可解释性方法的多样化，关键问题悬而未决：架构特征（如注意力机制）是否能改善可解释性？可解释性方法能否跨临床任务泛化？现有基准测试工作往往缺乏可扩展性和可复现性，且未能系统性地考察可解释性在临床任务与模型架构交互中的变化。

Method: 作者提出了一个全面的基准，系统评估多种可解释性方法在不同临床预测任务和模型架构上的表现，并重点分析架构特征（如注意力）与可解释性方法在不同临床任务中的交互作用。为保障可复现性与可扩展性，所有实现均通过文档完善的PyHealth开源框架发布。

Result: （1）合理利用的注意力机制是高效且能忠实解释模型预测的方法；（2）诸如KernelSHAP和LIME的黑盒解释器在时间序列临床预测任务中计算成本过高，实际不可行；（3）部分可解释性方法可靠性不足，难以令人信任。基于这些发现，作者提出了若干改进临床预测模型可解释性的指导建议。

Conclusion: 研究强调，可解释性方法的选择需结合具体临床任务和模型架构，注意力机制在恰当使用时具有显著优势，而部分主流黑盒方法在临床时间序列场景下存在实际应用障碍。作者呼吁未来的临床预测模型设计应优先考虑可解释性，并借助开源基准和框架推动该领域的标准化与可信性提升。

Abstract: Clinical decisions are high-stakes and require explicit justification, making model interpretability essential for auditing deep clinical models prior to deployment. As the ecosystem of model architectures and explainability methods expands, critical questions remain: Do architectural features like attention improve explainability? Do interpretability approaches generalize across clinical tasks? While prior benchmarking efforts exist, they often lack extensibility and reproducibility, and critically, fail to systematically examine how interpretability varies across the interplay of clinical tasks and model architectures. To address these gaps, we present a comprehensive benchmark evaluating interpretability methods across diverse clinical prediction tasks and model architectures. Our analysis reveals that: (1) attention when leveraged properly is a highly efficient approach for faithfully interpreting model predictions; (2) black-box interpreters like KernelSHAP and LIME are computationally infeasible for time-series clinical prediction tasks; and (3) several interpretability approaches are too unreliable to be trustworthy. From our findings, we discuss several guidelines on improving interpretability within clinical predictive pipelines. To support reproducibility and extensibility, we provide our implementations via PyHealth, a well-documented open-source framework: https://github.com/sunlabuiuc/PyHealth.

</details>


### [96] [Flow matching on homogeneous spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24829)
*Francesco Ruscelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将流匹配扩展到齐性空间的一般框架，通过将问题提升到李群上并简化为李代数上的欧氏流匹配任务，避免了黎曼流匹配中预度量或测地线的定义与计算，实现了更简单、更快速且完全内在的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黎曼流匹配方法在处理齐性空间时，需要定义并计算预度量和测地线，这涉及到复杂的几何结构且计算成本较高。作者旨在简化齐性空间上的流匹配流程，消除对复杂度量的依赖。

Method: 提出了一种针对齐性空间（李群的商空间）的流匹配框架。核心策略是将数据分布提升到底层的李群上，从而将齐性空间上的流匹配问题转化为李群上的问题。进一步利用李代数的线性结构，将问题简化为李代数上的欧氏流匹配任务，避开了齐性空间复杂的几何性质。

Result: 该方法成功实现了在齐性空间上的流匹配，且与黎曼流匹配相比，无需定义和计算预度量或测地线，框架更简洁，计算速度更快，并保持了完全的内在性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为齐性空间上的生成式建模提供了一种更高效、更简洁的替代方案，拓展了流匹配方法的应用范围，并通过避免复杂的几何计算降低了实现难度。

Abstract: We propose a general framework to extend Flow Matching to homogeneous spaces, i.e. quotients of Lie groups. Our approach reformulates the problem as a flow matching task on the underlying Lie group by lifting the data distributions. This strategy avoids the potentially complicated geometry of homogeneous spaces by working directly on Lie groups, which in turn enables us reduce the problem to a Euclidean flow matching task on Lie algebras. In contrast to Riemannian Flow Matching, our method eliminates the need to define and compute premetrics or geodesics, resulting in a simpler, faster, and fully intrinsic framework.

</details>


### [97] [Reaching Beyond the Mode: RL for Distributional Reasoning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24844)
*Isha Puri,Mehul Damani,Idan Shenfeld,Marzyeh Ghassemi,Jacob Andreas,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多答案强化学习方法，使语言模型能够在一次前向传播中显式生成多个候选答案，并分配置信度，从而在存在多种合理答案的不确定场景下提供了一种比推理时采样更高效的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型后训练过程（如RLHF）倾向于将答案分布坍缩为单一主导模式，这导致模型在面对医疗诊断、歧义问答、信息不完整等具有内在不确定性或多重合理答案的现实任务时表现不佳。理想情况下，模型应能生成多个合理的假设并附带置信度估计，而非依赖计算密集的重复采样。

Method: 提出多答案强化学习（Multi-answer RL）方法，通过修改强化学习目标，使模型能够在单次前向传播中显式生成多个候选答案，并将推理时的搜索行为内化到模型的生成过程中，从而避免重复采样带来的高昂计算成本。

Result: 在问答、医疗诊断和编程基准测试上，相比单答案训练基线，该方法在多样性、覆盖率和集合级校准分数上均有提升；相比竞争方法，生成多个答案所需token数更少；在编程任务上准确率显著提高。

Conclusion: 多答案强化学习是一种原则性强且计算高效的方案，可作为推理时扩展方法（如best-of-k）的替代，适用于需要处理多重合理答案与不确定性推理的现实任务场景。

Abstract: Given a question, a language model (LM) implicitly encodes a distribution over possible answers. In practice, post-training procedures for LMs often collapse this distribution onto a single dominant mode. While this is generally not a problem for benchmark-style evaluations that assume one correct answer, many real-world tasks inherently involve multiple valid answers or irreducible uncertainty. Examples include medical diagnosis, ambiguous question answering, and settings with incomplete information. In these cases, we would like LMs to generate multiple plausible hypotheses, ideally with confidence estimates for each one, and without computationally intensive repeated sampling to generate non-modal answers. This paper describes a multi-answer reinforcement learning approach for training LMs to perform distributional reasoning over multiple answers during inference. We modify the RL objective to enable models to explicitly generate multiple candidate answers in a single forward pass, internalizing aspects of inference-time search into the model's generative process. Across question-answering, medical diagnostic, and coding benchmarks, we observe improved diversity, coverage, and set-level calibration scores compared to single answer trained baselines. Models trained with our approach require fewer tokens to generate multiple answers than competing approaches. On coding tasks, they are also substantially more accurate. These results position multi-answer RL as a principled and compute-efficient alternative to inference-time scaling procedures such as best-of-k. Code and more information can be found at https://multi-answer-rl.github.io/.

</details>


### [98] [Learning to Staff: Offline Reinforcement Learning and Fine-Tuned LLMs for Warehouse Staffing Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24883)
*Kalle Kujanpää,Yuying Zhu,Kristina Klinkner,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比研究了两种机器学习方法用于优化半自动化仓库分拣系统的实时人员配置决策：基于Transformer的离线强化学习方法在详细状态表示下实现了2.4%的吞吐量提升；基于LLM的方法在抽象状态描述下，通过监督微调和DPO达到了与历史基线相当或略优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半自动化仓库分拣系统的实时人员配置决策优化具有重要的实际意义。不同的决策支持抽象层级具有不同的权衡，需要探索适合不同操作场景的AI辅助决策方法，以支持仓库管理者在详细数据和高层摘要之间做出有效决策。

Method: 研究评估了两种方法：1）基于Transformer的离线强化学习策略，使用详细历史状态表示进行训练；2）LLM方法，在抽象的、人类可读的状态描述上操作。对LLM方法系统比较了提示技术、自动提示优化和微调策略，最终采用监督微调结合模拟器生成的偏好进行直接偏好优化（DPO）。两种方法均在相应的模拟环境中评估。

Result: 离线RL方法在学习的模拟器中实现了比历史基线2.4%的吞吐量提升。在高吞吐量仓库运营中，这种规模的改进可转化为显著的成本节约。LLM方法中，仅使用提示不足以达到目标，但监督微调结合DPO在手工制作的模拟器中达到了匹配或略超历史基线的性能。

Conclusion: 两种方法都为AI辅助运营决策提供了可行路径。离线RL在任务特定架构下表现优异；LLM支持人类可读输入，可结合迭代反馈循环纳入管理者偏好。研究发现证明了针对不同决策层级选择合适方法的重要性。

Abstract: We investigate machine learning approaches for optimizing real-time staffing decisions in semi-automated warehouse sortation systems. Operational decision-making can be supported at different levels of abstraction, with different trade-offs. We evaluate two approaches, each in a matching simulation environment. First, we train custom Transformer-based policies using offline reinforcement learning on detailed historical state representations, achieving a 2.4% throughput improvement over historical baselines in learned simulators. In high-volume warehouse operations, improvements of this size translate to significant savings. Second, we explore LLMs operating on abstracted, human-readable state descriptions.
